# Prototype against VCFO Suite: what is there and what is missing

Written in ASD-STE100. One fact in each cell. Audit of the prototype `design/spectrum` at 2c978c5 against VCFO Suite `main` at 4794c75 (deployed), on 29 Oct 2026.

Four read-only audits compared the markup, the classes and the CSS values on both sides, one visible element or behaviour in each row. The hub checked the greeting-card gradient in the built CSS and corrected the rows that said otherwise. No browser check was made, so a row can still be wrong where the code and the screen differ.

**Key:** — = removed by the owner (fees, 30 Oct 2026; not counted). ✓ = present and the same. ◐ = present, but different (the row says how). ✗ = missing. "no data" = the real tool stores nothing that the part can show. "real only" or "Extra" = only VCFO Suite has it.

## Totals

After the fix rounds of 29 and 30 Oct 2026 (commits d5792c0 to a02a263 on `ui/spectrum`). The first audit
numbers are in brackets.

| Area | Rows | ✓ | ◐ | ✗ |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Foundations, shared parts and the shell | 380 | 302 (176) | 78 (156) | 0 (48) |
| Client portal | 907 | 719 (304) | 188 (540) | 0 (63) |
| Staff screens: project lead, manager, admin | 818 | 601 (189) | 217 (456) | 0 (173) |
| Super admin, developer console, public pages, shared staff screens | 645 (2 removed) | 421 (174) | 219 (330) | 3 (141) |
| **All** | **2750 (2 removed)** | **2043 (843)** | **702 (1482)** | **3 (425)** |

The status column now carries the result of the fix rounds. Each area's agent report gives the
reason for every row that is still ◐ or ✗ (no stored data, a real feature kept on purpose, an
owner decision, or not reached). The reports are in `docs/ui-port/fixes/fix-*.md` and, for the ✗ round of 30 Oct, `xfix-*.md`. A ◐ row whose
report says "real only", "kept" or "stays" is a deliberate difference, not a gap. No browser check
was made during the fix rounds.

## The greeting-card gradient (F-018, F-043, F-044, C-008, C-009, S-001, S-002)

- The colour values are the same as the prototype in light and dark mode (`--hero-gradient` in `app/globals.css`).
- `tailwind.config.ts` also has `backgroundImage: { hero: "var(--hero-gradient)", wash: "var(--hero-gradient)" }`. This makes a second `.bg-wash` rule that sets `background-image`, and it comes later in the built CSS.
- The value ends in a bare colour (`#e9e7ff`, dark `#15132e`). `background-image` does not accept a bare colour, so the browser drops every gradient layer.
- Result: every home card (client, lead, manager, admin, super admin, developer) is flat lavender in light mode and flat dark violet in dark mode.
- Fix: remove `hero` and `wash` from `backgroundImage` in `tailwind.config.ts`. Done in d5792c0.

## The most visible gaps

| Area | Gap | Rows |
|---|---|---|
| All homes | No gradient on the home card. | F-043, C-008, S-001 |
| All homes | No small text after the tile number ("/ 6", "+2"). Some tiles show the default grid icon. The date reads "Wednesday · 29 Oct 2026 · 17:47", not "Wed 29 Oct 2026 · 12:31 PM". | F-048, F-064, P-015 |
| Lead, manager, admin homes | No trend lines in the tiles. No data: step states, task completion and stage changes keep no history. | S-040, S-073, S-100 |
| Shell | No rail and no tab bar from 768 to 1023 px. No client, lead or manager folds in the sidebar. Red count badges almost never show. No sign-out in the profile row. | F-273, F-284, F-288 to F-291, F-303, F-307 |
| Shell | Ask VCFO is missing for the lead, the manager and the developer. Staff search is a dropdown, not the centred dialog with groups. | F-298, F-299, F-322, F-324 |
| Dialogs | Black 80 % scrim, not navy 42 %. No 52 px head bar. No bottom sheet on phones. The side panel is 384 px, not 440 px. | F-105, F-106, F-109, F-110 |
| Manager | No Work page with the planner, Assign and Reassign. No page for one project lead. | S-231 to S-238, S-359 to S-373 |
| Admin | No Managers grid with escalations and leads without a manager. | S-380 to S-396 |
| Staff records | No Timeline card (Gantt) on any staff client record. No Steps card on the manager and admin record. No "What's this?" or Ask panel. | S-433 to S-441, S-451 |
| Staff calendar | No Timeline view with drag and resize of filing windows. | S-673, S-674 |
| Client portal | The old drill dialog and toast still use the v7 look and do not change in dark mode. | C-084, C-085, C-136 |
| Client portal | "Not started" chips are blue, not grey (`toneFor` matches "started"). | C-452 |
| Client portal | No upload dialog with a request list and drop zone. No "Filings by month" chart. No Profile page with the 220 px section menu. | C-059, C-342, C-813 |
| Super admin | The attention list has no date tile and no Open action. The Overview has no "Registrations open" tile and no Practice areas grid. The client record has no Gantt. | P-034, P-010, P-090 |
| Developer | Three Overview tiles are missing. No write actions (rotate key, disconnect, roll back): every developer route only reads. | P-273, P-305 |
| Shared staff screens | No document viewer and no knowledge article reader for staff. Vault rows have no "…" menu. | P-601, P-565, P-587 |
| Public | "Book a demo" is a separate page in the older look, not the dialog. Sign-in has no demo account buttons. Landing screenshots cannot be clicked. | P-388, P-443, P-396 |

## Defects found on the way

| Defect | Where |
|---|---|
| The greeting-card "skin" picker does nothing: it sets `--hero-surface-bg`, which only the unused `.lead-hero` class reads. | S-015, `src/lib/shell/shell-appearance.ts` |
| The Risks Age column says "1 days". | `src/views/super/SuperRisks.tsx` (`ageLabel`) |
| The "Submitted" chip on the manager and admin engagement page uses raw emerald colours and stays light in dark mode. | `src/views/incorporation/MilestoneResponseRowSummary.tsx:50` |
| The step page head never shows its status chip, because `internWorkspace` is always true. | `src/views/engagement/EngagementStepDetail.tsx:402` |
| The manager Projects board shows a city made from a hash of the engagement id. That is not real data. | `src/views/admin/Projects.tsx:22` |
| Email, Analytics and Profile show a back arrow, which R-09 forbids. Vault, Audit log and Profile repeat the page title (R-09). | S-583, S-615, P-527, P-571 |
| The card icon table lacks words such as pipeline, capacity, firm profile, session and access, so those cards show the default icon. | `src/lib/ui/card-head-icon.ts` |
